# 简体中文 (zh): reviewed translations

Every entry of `locale/zh/LC_MESSAGES/strings.po` that this change replaces. "Old" is the translation in the repository before this change (marked *fuzzy* when the program did not use it), "New" the reviewed one. Approve or correct each row in `zh.csv` (columns *Approved* and *Translator note*). See [README.md](README.md) for how the changes were made.

| Type | Count |
|---|---|
| format | 3 |
| corrected | 743 |
| fuzzy corrected | 85 |
| fuzzy confirmed | 10 |
| new | 167 |
| manual | 1 |
| **Total** | **1009** |

| 1 | corrected | Index.<br>The rows in gray color will populate the Bookmarks menu.<br>The number of gray colored rows is set in Preferences. | 行。<br>灰色行将填充书签菜单。<br>灰色行的数量设置在首选项中。 | 索引。<br>灰色行将填充书签菜单。<br>灰色行的数量在首选项中设置。 | "Index" was translated as "row" (行); the column is the index (#) column. | ☐ |
| 2 | corrected | Description of the link that is set as an menu action.<br>Try to keep it short because it is installed as a menu item. | 描述将链接设置为菜单操作。<br>尽量保持短，因为它被安装为菜单项。 | 设置为菜单动作的链接的描述。<br>请尽量简短，因为它会作为菜单项显示。 | Broken grammar ("describe sets the link as menu action"); reworded as a description of the link. | ☐ |

| 4 | corrected | Backup Site | 备份位置 | 备用站点 | "Site" means a website here (bookmark), not a location (位置). — reviewer: Right idea (a website, not a location), but 备份站点 suggests a site that stores backups; the entry opens an 'Alternative website', so 备用站点 is the accurate term. The default bookmark title is re-set from the translation on every install_bookmarks() call, so the change is safe. | ☐ |

C4 = 带 x 个刃的圆形刀具<br>B = 球头铣刀<br>V = V 形铣刀<br>L = 激光 | Fuzzy: added the missing 'L = laser' line; 'x flutes' rendered as cutting edges (刃) instead of grooves; fixed '。。。' artifact. | ☐ |

| 17 | corrected | - Isolation -> informative - lower Feedrate as it uses a milling bit with a fine tip.<br>- Roughing  -> informative - lower Feedrate and multiDepth cut.<br>- Finishing -> informative - higher Feedrate, without multiDepth.<br>- Polish -> adds a painting sequence over the whole area of the object | - 隔离 -> 信息 - 降低进给率，因为它使用带有细尖的铣刀。<br>- 粗加工 -> 信息 - 降低进给率和多深度切削。<br>- 精加工 -> 信息丰富 - 更高的进给率，没有多深度。<br>- 修饰 -> 在对象的整个区域添加绘画序列 | - 隔离 -> 仅供参考 - 较低进给率，因为使用细尖铣刀。<br>- 粗加工 -> 仅供参考 - 较低进给率并采用多深度切削。<br>- 精加工 -> 仅供参考 - 较高进给率，不使用多深度。<br>- 抛光 -> 在对象的整个区域上添加一个绘制序列 | "informative" was translated as "information / information-rich" (信息/信息丰富); "Polish" was 修饰, inconsistent with 抛光 used for the Polishing job; "painting" should be 绘制 (Paint plugin). — reviewer: Proposal accepted (抛光 matches the 'Polishing' combo item) except the Roughing line: '并多深度切削' lacks a verb, changed to '并采用多深度切削'. | ☐ |
| 18 | corrected | Tool Offset.<br>Can be of a few types:<br>Path = zero offset<br>In = offset inside by half of tool diameter<br>Out = offset outside by half of tool diameter<br>Custom = custom offset using the Custom Offset value | 刀具偏移。<br>可以有几种类型：<br>路径=零偏移<br>In=工具直径一半的内部偏移量<br>Out=工具直径的一半向外偏移<br>自定义=使用自定义偏移值的自定义偏移 | 刀具偏移。<br>可以有几种类型：<br>路径 = 零偏移<br>内 = 向内偏移刀具直径的一半<br>外 = 向外偏移刀具直径的一半<br>自定义 = 使用“自定义偏移量”值进行偏移 | "In"/"Out" left in English while the combo shows 内/外; tool should be 刀具. | ☐ |
| 19 | corrected | Multi Depth.<br>Selecting this will allow cutting in multiple passes,<br>each pass adding a DPP parameter depth. | 多深度。<br>选择此选项将允许在多个焊道中进行切割，<br>每次通过添加一个DPP参数深度。 | 多深度。<br>选择此项将允许分多次走刀切削，<br>每次走刀增加DPP（每刀深度）参数设定的深度。 | Wrong domain term: "pass" was translated as 焊道 (welding bead). — reviewer: Pass term fixed as proposed; last line reworded ('一个DPP参数深度' lacked 的) and links DPP to the new label 每刀深度. | ☐ |
| 20 | corrected | DPP | 每次通过的深度 | 每刀深度 | "Depth per pass" was a literal "每次通过的深度"; 每刀深度 is the CNC term used elsewhere (每刀的深度). | ☐ |
| 21 | corrected | DPP. Depth per Pass.<br>The value used to cut into material on each pass. | DPP.每次通过的深度。<br>用于在每次焊道上切割材料的值。 | DPP：每刀深度。<br>每次走刀切入材料的深度值。 | Wrong domain term: "pass" was translated as 焊道 (welding bead). | ☐ |
| 22 | corrected | Clearance Height.<br>Height at which the milling bit will travel between cuts,<br>above the surface of the material, avoiding all fixtures. | 安全高度。<br>铣削钻头在切削之间移动的高度，<br>在材料表面上方，避免所有固定装置。 | 安全高度。<br>铣刀在两次切削之间移动时的高度，<br>位于材料表面上方，避开所有夹具。 | "milling bit" was translated as 铣削钻头 (drill bit); fixtures are 夹具. | ☐ |
| 23 | corrected | Extra Cut.<br>If checked, after a isolation is finished an extra cut<br>will be added where the start and end of isolation meet<br>such as that this point is covered by this extra cut to<br>ensure a complete isolation. | 额外切割<br>如果选中，隔离完成后，将在隔离起点和终点相交处添加额外切口，例如，该点被该额外切口覆盖，以确保完全隔离。 | 额外切割。<br>如果选中，隔离完成后将在隔离的起点<br>和终点交汇处添加一段额外切割，<br>使该点被这段额外切割覆盖，<br>以确保完全隔离。 | "such as that" (= so that) was mistranslated as "for example" (例如); line breaks restored. | ☐ |

| 28 | corrected | The 'Operation' can be:<br>- Isolation -> will ensure that the non-copper clearing is always complete.<br>If it's not successful then the non-copper clearing will fail, too.<br>- Clear -> the regular non-copper clearing. | “操作”可以是：<br>-隔离->将确保始终完成非盗铜。<br>如果不成功，那么非铜清算也将失败。<br>-清除->常规非盗铜。 | “操作”可以是：<br>- 隔离 -> 确保非铜清除始终完整。<br>如果隔离不成功，非铜清除也将失败。<br>- 清除 -> 常规的非铜清除。 | Wrong terms: non-copper clearing was 盗铜 (copper thieving) and 清算 (financial liquidation); glossary: Non-Copper Clearing = 非铜清除. | ☐ |
| 29 | corrected | Milling type:<br>- climb / best for precision milling and to reduce tool usage<br>- conventional / useful when there is no backlash compensation | 铣削类型：<br>-爬升/最佳精密铣削和减少刀具使用<br>-常规/在没有齿隙补偿时有用 | 铣削类型：<br>- 顺铣 / 最适合精密铣削并减少刀具磨损<br>- 逆铣 / 在没有反向间隙补偿时有用 | Wrong domain terms: climb/conventional milling are 顺铣/逆铣 (爬升 = "climbing up"); "tool usage" means tool wear; backlash = 反向间隙. | ☐ |

Default search time is 3sec.<br>- Basic -> Using Google OR-Tools Basic algorithm<br>- TSA -> Using Travelling Salesman algorithm<br><br>Some options are disabled when the application works in 32bit mode. | 这设置了路径优化算法。<br>-R树->Rtree算法（快速进行空间检索）<br>-元启发式（MetaHeuristic）-> 使用带有MetaHeuristic与谷歌OR-Tools算法。默认搜索时间为3秒。<br>-基本->谷歌OR-Tools基本算法<br>-囊状群（TSA）->使用移动优化算法<br>当应用程序在32位模式下工作时，某些选项被禁用。 | 这将设置路径优化算法。<br>- R树 -> Rtree算法<br>- 元启发式 -> 使用Google OR-Tools算法，<br>采用元启发式引导局部搜索。默认搜索时间为3秒。<br>- 基本 -> 使用Google OR-Tools基本算法<br>- TSA -> 使用旅行商算法<br><br>当应用程序以32位模式运行时，某些选项不可用。 | TSA (Travelling Salesman Algorithm) was translated as the meaningless 囊状群 / 'move optimization algorithm'. | 483 | corrected | - 'Itself' - the copper thieving extent is based on the object extent.<br>- 'Area Selection' - left mouse click to start selection of the area to be filled.<br>- 'Reference Object' - will do copper thieving within the area specified by another object. | - '自身' -盗铜程度是基于对象范围。<br>-“区域选择”-鼠标左键点击开始选择要填充的区域。<br>“参考对象”-会在另一个对象指定的区域内清除铜。 | - '自身' - 盗铜范围基于对象的范围。<br>- '区域选择' - 单击鼠标左键开始选择要填充的区域。<br>- '参考对象' - 在另一个对象指定的区域内进行盗铜。 | 'extent' rendered as '程度' (degree); last option said 'clear copper' instead of copper thieving; missing '- ' mark, mixed quotes | ☐ |

| 486 | corrected | - 'Rectangular' - the bounding box will be of rectangular shape.<br>- 'Minimal' - the bounding box will be the convex hull shape. | '矩形' -包围框将是矩形形状。<br>“最小”-包围盒将是凸包形状。 | - '矩形' - 边界框为矩形。<br>- '最小' - 边界框为凸包形状。 | Missing '- ' list marks and mixed quotes; '包围框'/'包围盒' unified to the glossary term '边界框' | ☐ |
| 487 | corrected | - 'Solid' - copper thieving will be a solid polygon.<br>- 'Dots Grid' - the empty area will be filled with a pattern of dots.<br>- 'Squares Grid' - the empty area will be filled with a pattern of squares.<br>- 'Lines Grid' - the empty area will be filled with a pattern of lines. | -'实心' -盗铜将是一个实心多边形。<br>-“点网络”-空白区域将填充一个图案的点。<br>-“方格网络”-空白处将被方格图案填充。<br>-“线型网络”-空区域将被线条图案填充。 | - '实心' - 盗铜为一个实心多边形。<br>- '点网格' - 空白区域将以点阵图案填充。<br>- '方格网格' - 空白区域将以方格图案填充。<br>- '线网格' - 空白区域将以线条图案填充。 | 'Grid' written as '网络' (network); option names now match the option labels | ☐ |

| 490 | corrected | Dots Grid Parameters | 点网络参数 | 点网格参数 | '网络' means network; 'Grid' is '网格' (as in '点网格') | ☐ |

Can be:<br>- Fixed Diameter -> all holes will have a set size<br>- Fixed Annular Ring -> all holes will have a set annular ring<br>- Proportional -> each hole size will be a fraction of the pad size | 处理焊盘的方法。可以是：<br>-固定直径->所有孔都有一个设定的尺寸<br>-固定环->所有孔都有一个固定环<br>-比例->每个孔尺寸将是焊盘尺寸的一小部分 | 处理焊盘的方法。可以是：<br>- 固定直径 -> 所有孔都使用设定的尺寸<br>- 固定孔环 -> 所有孔都保留设定的孔环<br>- 比例 -> 每个孔的尺寸为焊盘尺寸的一定比例 | Option name fixed ('固定环' = retaining ring -> '固定孔环'); 'fraction' rendered as '一小部分' (small part) | ☐ |

| 504 | corrected | This set the fiducial diameter if fiducial type is circular,<br>otherwise is the size of the fiducial.<br>The soldermask opening is double than that. | 如果基准类型为圆形，则设置基准直径，<br>否则为基准点的大小。<br>焊盘开口比那个大一倍。 | 如果基准类型为圆形，则设置基准直径，<br>否则为基准点的大小。<br>阻焊层开口为该值的两倍。 | 'soldermask opening' mistranslated as '焊盘开口' (pad opening) — reviewer: Fix is right; '阻焊层开口' matches glossary ('Add Soldermask Opening' = '添加阻焊层开口') and '该值' is clearer than '其' | ☐ |

| 508 | corrected | The punch hole source can be:<br>- Excellon Object-> the Excellon object drills center will serve as reference.<br>- Fixed Diameter -> will try to use the pads center as reference adding fixed diameter holes.<br>- Fixed Annular Ring -> will try to keep a set annular ring.<br>- Proportional -> will make a Gerber punch hole having the diameter a percentage of the pad diameter. | 打孔源可以是：<br>-Excellon对象->Excellon 对象钻孔中心将作为参考。<br>-固定直径->将尝试使用焊盘中心作为添加固定直径孔的参考。<br>-固定环->将尝试保持固定环。<br>-比例->将制作一个直径为焊盘直径百分比的Gerber打孔。 | 打孔源可以是：<br>- Excellon对象 -> 以Excellon对象的钻孔中心作为参考。<br>- 固定直径 -> 尝试以焊盘中心为参考，添加固定直径的孔。<br>- 固定孔环 -> 尝试保持设定的孔环。<br>- 比例 -> 制作直径为焊盘直径一定百分比的Gerber打孔。 | 'Fixed Annular Ring' rendered as '固定环' (retaining ring), annular ring is '孔环'; sentences smoothed | ☐ |
| 509 | corrected | Parameter that controls the error correction used for the QR Code.<br>L = maximum 7%% errors can be corrected<br>M = maximum 15%% errors can be corrected<br>Q = maximum 25%% errors can be corrected<br>H = maximum 30%% errors can be corrected. | 参数，用于控制用于二维码的错误更正。<br>L=最多可更正7%%个错误<br>M=最多可更正15%%的错误<br>Q=最多可纠正25%%的错误<br>H=最多可更正30%%的错误。 | 用于控制二维码纠错级别的参数。<br>L = 最多可纠正 7%% 的错误<br>M = 最多可纠正 15%% 的错误<br>Q = 最多可纠正 25%% 的错误<br>H = 最多可纠正 30%% 的错误。 | Clumsy machine wording ('参数，用于控制用于...'), '7%%个错误' is ungrammatical, and the verb switched between 更正 and 纠正. | 510 | corrected | Size of the QRCode border. How many boxes thick is the border.<br>Default value is 4. The width of the clearance around the QRCode. | 二维码边框的大小。边界有多厚。<br>默认值为4。二维码周围的间隙宽度。 | 二维码边框的大小，即边框的厚度为几个框。<br>默认值为4。即二维码周围空白区域的宽度。 | The translation dropped 'how many boxes' thick: the border is measured in boxes. — reviewer: Right idea (border measured in boxes, 框 matches 'Box Size' = 框尺寸), but '有多少个框的厚度' is awkward; reworded to '边框的厚度为几个框'. | ☐ |
| 511 | corrected | Choose the polarity of the QRCode.<br>It can be drawn in a negative way (squares are clear)<br>or in a positive way (squares are opaque). | 选择二维码的极性。<br>它可以以负向方式（正方形是透明的）或正向方式（正方形是不透明的）绘制。 | 选择二维码的极性。<br>可以按负片方式绘制（方块透明），<br>也可以按正片方式绘制（方块不透明）。 | '负向方式/正向方式' is a literal machine translation. Uses the glossary terms 负片/正片 (negative/positive film) and keeps the line breaks. | ☐ |
| 512 | corrected | Choose the type of QRCode to be created.<br>If added on a Silkscreen Gerber file the QRCode may<br>be added as positive. If it is added to a Copper Gerber<br>file then perhaps the QRCode can be added as negative. | 选择要创建的二维码的类型。<br>如果在Gerber丝印中添加二维码，则二维码可以添加为正值。如果将其添加到覆铜板Gerber中，则二维码可能可以添加为负数。 | 选择要创建的二维码类型。<br>如果添加到丝印Gerber文件中，二维码<br>可以按正片添加。如果添加到铜层Gerber<br>文件中，二维码或许可以按负片添加。 | Wrong meaning: positive/negative were rendered as '正值/负数' (positive value/negative number) and the copper layer as '覆铜板' (copper-clad board). Now uses 正片/负片 and 铜层. | ☐ |
| 513 | corrected | Trace Size | 跟踪大小 | 走线宽度 | Mistranslated PCB term: 'trace' means a copper track (走线), not 'tracking' (跟踪). | ☐ |

| 515 | corrected | Minimum acceptable trace size. | 可接受的最小跟踪大小。 | 可接受的最小走线宽度。 | '跟踪' (tracking) is the wrong word for a PCB trace. Should be 走线. | ☐ |
| 516 | corrected | Copper to Outline clearance | 铜的轮廓间隙 | 铜与轮廓间隙 | Ungrammatical ('copper's outline clearance'). Now matches the sibling label 丝印与轮廓间间隙. | ☐ |
| 517 | corrected | Minimum Annular Ring | 最小环形环规则 | 最小焊环 | '环形环规则' is redundant and adds a 'rule' word that the source does not have. The standard term for annular ring is 焊环. | ☐ |

The source means the holes stay in place (保持原位). | ☐ |
| 522 | corrected | The coordinates used as reference for the mirror operation.<br>Can be:<br>- Point -> a set of coordinates (x,y) around which the object is mirrored<br>- Box -> a set of coordinates (x, y) obtained from the center of the<br>bounding box of another object selected below<br>- Snap -> a point defined by the center of a drill hole in a Excellon object | 用作镜像操作参考的坐标。<br>可以是：<br>-点->对象镜像所围绕的一组坐标（x，y）<br>-框->从下面选择的另一个对象的边界框中心获得的一组坐标（x，y）<br>-- 折断->由Excellon对象中钻孔中心定义的点 | 用作镜像操作参考的坐标。<br>可以是：<br>- 点 -> 对象围绕其镜像的一组坐标 (x,y)<br>- 框 -> 从下方所选另一对象的<br>边界框中心获得的一组坐标 (x,y)<br>- 捕捉 -> 由Excellon对象中某个钻孔的中心定义的点 | 'Snap' was mistranslated as '折断' (break). The UI option is 捕捉. Also fixes the stray '--' and restores the line breaks. | ☐ |
| 523 | corrected | This is the board area. | 这是板子的区域。 | 这是电路板的面积。 | Wrong meaning: 'area' here is surface area (面积), not a region (区域). | ☐ |
| 524 | corrected | Current density to pass through the board. <br>In Amps per Square Feet ASF. | 通过电路板的电流密度。<br>以每平方英尺ASF的安培数为单位。 | 通过电路板的电流密度。<br>单位为安培每平方英尺 (ASF)。 | Garbled unit phrase ('以每平方英尺ASF的安培数为单位'). Now states the unit clearly as amps per square foot (ASF). | ☐ |
| 525 | corrected | Diameter of the tool used to cutout<br>the PCB shape out of the surrounding material. | 用于从周围材料中切割PCB形状的工具的直径。 | 用于从周围材料中切割出<br>PCB外形的刀具直径。 | Cutting tool should be 刀具, as in the glossary 'Tool Diameter' = 刀具直径, not the generic 工具. Line break restored. | ☐ |
| 526 | corrected | Kind | 方法 | 类型 | Wrong meaning: 'Kind' picks the object type (单个/拼板). It is not a method (方法). | ☐ |
| 527 | corrected | Mouse Bytes | 鼠标字节 | 邮票孔 | Wrong meaning: '鼠标字节' means 'computer mouse bytes'. The source label is a typo for 'Mouse Bites', the PCB break-off perforation, which in Chinese is 邮票孔. | ☐ |
| 528 | corrected | Number of gaps used for the cutout.<br>There can be maximum 8 bridges/gaps.<br>The choices are:<br>- None  - no gaps<br>- lr    - left + right<br>- tb    - top + bottom<br>- 4     - left + right +top + bottom<br>- 2lr   - 2*left + 2*right<br>- 2tb  - 2*top + 2*bottom<br>- 8     - 2*left + 2*right +2*top + 2*bottom | 用于剪切的间隙数。<br>最多可以有8个桥/间隙。<br>这些选择包括：<br>-无-无差距<br>-lr   -左后-左+右<br>-tb  -顶部+底部<br>-4  -左+右+上+下<br>-2lr  -2*左+2*右<br>-2tb  -2*顶部+2*底部<br>-8  -2*左+2*右+2*顶+2*底 | 用于切割的缺口数量。<br>最多可有8个桥接/缺口。<br>可选项：<br>- None  - 无缺口<br>- lr    - 左 + 右<br>- tb    - 上 + 下<br>- 4     - 左 + 右 + 上 + 下<br>- 2lr   - 2*左 + 2*右<br>- 2tb  - 2*上 + 2*下<br>- 8     - 2*左 + 2*右 + 2*上 + 2*下 | Garbled items: '无差距' means 'no disparity' and '左后-左+右' is broken.
